    90	/**
    91	 * Preflight before a child runs in a seat: the worktree is listed, its index
    92	 * is clean, and HEAD sits on the expected branch. Refusals name the failed
    93	 * check and never issue destructive git.
    94	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string}} target @param {any} io
    95	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, check: string, refusal: string}>}
    96	 */
    97	export async function preflightWorktree(root, target, io) {
    98	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
    99	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
   100	  if (!listed.some((e) => e.path === path)) {
   101	    return { ok: false, check: 'missing-worktree', refusal: `worktree ${path} is not listed by git — run setup or self-heal first` };
   102	  }
   103	  const status = (await git(io, path, ['status', '--porcelain'])).stdout.trim();
   104	  if (status.length > 0) {
   105	    return { ok: false, check: 'dirty-index', refusal: `worktree ${path} has a dirty index/working tree — a clean seat is required before a child runs` };
   106	  }
   107	  const head = (await git(io, path, ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'])).stdout.trim();
   108	  if (head !== target.branch) {
   109	    return { ok: false, check: 'wrong-branch', refusal: `worktree ${path} HEAD is on '${head}', expected branch '${target.branch}'` };
   110	  }
   111	  return { ok: true };
   112	}
   113	
   114	/**
   115	 * Postflight after a child: HEAD still on the expected branch (checked in the
   116	 * seat cwd) and main unmoved from the pre-child capture.
   117	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string, mainSha: string}} target @param {any} io
   118	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, check: string, refusal: string}>}
   119	 */
   120	export async function postflightWorktree(root, target, io) {
   121	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
   122	  const head = (await git(io, path, ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'])).stdout.trim();
   123	  if (head !== target.branch) {
   124	    return { ok: false, check: 'head-drift', refusal: `the child left HEAD on '${head}' instead of the expected branch '${target.branch}'` };
   125	  }
   126	  const main = (await git(io, root, ['rev-parse', 'main'])).stdout.trim();
   127	  if (main !== target.mainSha) {
   128	    return { ok: false, check: 'main-moved', refusal: `main moved under the child (was ${target.mainSha}, now ${main}) — only the merger writes main` };
   129	  }
   130	  return { ok: true };
   131	}
   132

   134	 * Full-range attribution scan over main..branch — the merge gate. Every
   135	 * commit must be authored AND committed by the sole author, with no forbidden

   308	    // Recognition requires BOTH a supervisor-owned merge receipt AND the

   311	    // writer that crashed before its first commit), and a receipt alone can
   312	    // outlive a rewound main. Ancestor-without-receipt parks as stale;

   316	      await io.execFile('git', ['merge-base', '--is-ancestor', branch, 'main'], { cwd: root });

   370	    const superviseVerdictChild = async (c) => {
   371	      let attempt = 0;
   372	      /** @type {any | null} */
   373	      let forced = null;
   374	      const chainLen = Array.isArray(config?.roles?.[c.roleChain]) ? config.roles[c.roleChain].length : 1;
   375	      for (;;) {
   376	        const resolved = forced ?? resolveOne(c.roleChain, subtaskAvoidEntries);
   377	        if (!resolved) return { parked: await park(`no eligible ${c.roleLabel} remains for subtask '${c.subtaskId}' (every chain entry is avoided)`) };

   382	        const cls = classify({ text: transcriptTail(log), exitCode: result.exitCode ?? 0, platform: asg.platform, table }).class;
   383	        if (cls === 'ok') return { result };

   402	        });

   404	        if (decision.action === 'relaunch') {

   406	          forced = { ...decision.assignment, role: c.childRole };

   417	      // Writer (write-capable, its own seat). Every resolution — including a

   445	        const chainLen = Array.isArray(config?.roles?.[`worker-${seat}`]) ? config.roles[`worker-${seat}`].length : 1;
   446	        if (failoverAttempt > chainLen) {

   463	        });
   464	        if (decision.action === 'park') return park(`writer failover parked subtask '${st.id}': ${decision.reason}`);
   465	        if (decision.action === 'relaunch') {
   466	          // Carry entry-level avoidance across relaunches — a rejected model

   468	          if (Array.isArray(decision.avoidEntries)) subtaskAvoidEntries = decision.avoidEntries;
   469	          forcedWriter = { ...decision.assignment, role: `worker-${seat}` };

   476	        if (after.status === LOOP_STATUS.ESCALATED || (after.iterations?.[gate] ?? 0) >= cap) {
   477	          return escalate(gate, findings, cap);
   478	        }

   485	      // An empty branch never merges (Gate-2 fold G3): the writer must have

   487	      const ahead = String((await io.execFile('git', ['log', `main..${branch}`, '--format=%H'], { cwd: root })).stdout).trim();

   495	        if (reviewerPre.ok !== true) return park(`reviewer-seat preflight refused subtask '${st.id}': ${reviewerPre.refusal}`);

   499	
   500	      // Reviewer (read-only, the OTHER seat's model and cwd, subtask-reviewer role).

   504	        "End with exactly:\nVERDICT: APPROVED | APPROVED_WITH_NOTES | BLOCKED\nFINDINGS: numbered findings, or 'none'",
   505	      ].join('\n\n');
   506	      // A reviewer DEATH is never a review verdict (dogfood finding D6): a

   508	      // subtaskAvoidEntries) and relaunches, bounded by the chain length —
   509	      // only a real verdict reaches the gate.

  1218	// ===========================================================================
  1219	// ITEM 2 (v1.1) — trunk derivation. Replace hardcoded 'main' with the repo's
  1220	// actual default branch (git symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD → fallback
  1221	// rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD at setup), recorded in state and reused, never
  1222	// re-derived mid-run. Pinned in a MASTER-trunk fake repo — the observable is
  1223	// "master used everywhere; one derivation", not the exact command.
  1224	
  1225	// A fake git modeling a MASTER-trunk repo: the default branch is 'master'
  1226	// (symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD → origin/master; fallback rev-parse
  1227	// --abbrev-ref HEAD at the ROOT → master). It answers 'main' refs LENIENTLY
  1228	// (same sha/ranges) so a main-hardcoded impl still runs end to end — the pin's
  1229	// teeth are that NO 'main' appears in the recorded git argv. deriveThrows makes
  1230	// the default-branch derivation fail (to prove a resume reuses a stamped trunk).
  1231	function masterGit({ merged = [], existingBranches = [], dirtyCwds = [], log = CLEAN_LOG, deriveThrows = false } = {}) {
  1232	  const added = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map());
  1233	  const branchAt = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map());
  1234	  const branches = new Set(existingBranches);
  1235	  const dirty = new Set(dirtyCwds);
  1236	  const mergedSet = new Set(merged);
  1237	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
  1238	  const lockProbeRef = { fn: /** @type {null | (() => boolean)} */ (null) };
  1239	  const list = () => {
  1240	    const entries = [{ path: '/repo', branch: 'master' }];
  1241	    for (const [path, branch] of added) entries.push({ path, branch: branchAt.get(path) ?? branch });
  1242	    return entries.map((e) => `worktree ${e.path}\nHEAD ${MAIN_SHA}\nbranch refs/heads/${e.branch}\n`).join('\n');
  1243	  };
  1244	  const fn = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
  1245	    const argstr = args.join(' ');
  1246	    calls.push({ cmd, args, argstr, cwd: opts?.cwd, lockPresent: lockProbeRef.fn ? lockProbeRef.fn() : undefined });
  1247	    if (cmd !== 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
  1248	    // ---- trunk derivation (both forms) ----
  1249	    if (argstr === 'symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD') {
  1250	      if (deriveThrows) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('fatal: no origin/HEAD'), { code: 128, stderr: 'no origin/HEAD\n' }));
  1251	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'refs/remotes/origin/master\n', stderr: '' });
  1252	    }
  1253	    if (argstr === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD') {
  1254	      // LEGIT seat HEAD probes (alreadyOnBranch / preflight / postflight) run in
  1255	      // a seat cwd and return that seat's branch (never 'master' — a pre-checkout
  1256	      // probe yields the seat base, so it can never be mistaken for the trunk).
  1257	      if (opts?.cwd === WT_A || opts?.cwd === WT_B) {
  1258	        const dflt = opts.cwd === WT_A ? 'baton/wt-a/base' : 'baton/wt-b/base';
  1259	        return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${branchAt.get(opts.cwd) ?? dflt}\n`, stderr: '' });
  1260	      }
  1261	      // Anything else — the root fallback OR a STRAY probe in any other cwd — is
  1262	      // a trunk-derivation attempt: it fails under deriveThrows and is counted as
  1263	      // a derivation call (so a seat-cwd re-derivation cannot slip past).
  1264	      if (deriveThrows) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('fatal: HEAD'), { code: 128, stderr: 'fatal: not a HEAD\n' }));
  1265	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'master\n', stderr: '' });
  1266	    }
  1267	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree list')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: list(), stderr: '' });
  1268	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree add')) {
  1269	      const path = args.find((a) => a.startsWith('/repo/.worktrees/'));
  1270	      const bi = args.indexOf('-b');
  1271	      const branch = bi >= 0 ? args[bi + 1] : 'baton/wt-x/base';
  1272	      if (path) { added.set(path, branch); branchAt.set(path, branch); }
  1273	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
  1274	    }
  1275	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree prune') || argstr.startsWith('worktree remove')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
  1276	    if (argstr.startsWith('checkout -b')) {
  1277	      const target = args[args.indexOf('-b') + 1];
  1278	      if (branches.has(target)) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists`), { code: 128, stderr: `fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists\n` }));
  1279	      branches.add(target);
  1280	      if (opts?.cwd) branchAt.set(opts.cwd, target);
  1281	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
  1282	    }
  1283	    if (argstr.startsWith('status --porcelain')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: dirty.has(opts?.cwd) ? ' M dirty.txt\n' : '', stderr: '' });
  1284	    // merge-base --is-ancestor <branch> <trunk>: exit 0 iff branch already merged.
  1285	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge-base --is-ancestor')) {
  1286	      const b = args[args.indexOf('--is-ancestor') + 1];
  1287	      return mergedSet.has(b) ? Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }) : Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('not an ancestor'), { code: 1, stdout: '', stderr: '' }));
  1288	    }
  1289	    // Lenient: rev-parse of ANY single ref (master OR main) resolves to the sha.
  1290	    if (argstr.startsWith('rev-parse')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${MAIN_SHA}\n`, stderr: '' });
  1291	    if (argstr.startsWith('log ')) {
  1292	      const range = args.find((a) => /\.\./.test(String(a))) ?? '';
  1293	      const branch = String(range).split('..')[1];
  1294	      if (branch && mergedSet.has(branch)) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }); // merged → empty
  1295	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: log, stderr: '' });
  1296	    }
  1297	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'Fast-forward', stderr: '' }); // merge <branch> and merge --ff-only <trunk>
  1298	    if (argstr.startsWith('branch -D')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
  1299	    return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`unstubbed git: ${argstr}`), { code: 'ENOSTUB' }));
  1300	  };
  1301	  fn.calls = calls;
  1302	  fn.git = () => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git');
  1303	  fn.mainCalls = () => fn.git().filter((c) => c.argstr.includes('main'));
  1304	  // A trunk-derivation attempt = symbolic-ref origin/HEAD OR any abbrev-ref HEAD
  1305	  // that is NOT one of the known legit SEAT probes (WT_A/WT_B). This counts a
  1306	  // seat-cwd re-derivation too — the exclusion is the explicit seat allowlist,
  1307	  // never a blanket "non-root" filter — so "derive once, never re-derive" is
  1308	  // pinned by the count, not just the observable trunk.
  1309	  fn.derivationCalls = () =>
  1310	    fn.git().filter(
  1311	      (c) => c.argstr === 'symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD' || (c.argstr === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD' && c.cwd !== WT_A && c.cwd !== WT_B),
  1312	    ).length;
  1313	  // The legit seat HEAD probes the pipeline makes (alreadyOnBranch / preflight /
  1314	  // postflight), confined to the two seat cwds — asserted explicitly.
  1315	  fn.seatProbes = () => fn.git().filter((c) => c.argstr === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD' && (c.cwd === WT_A || c.cwd === WT_B));
  1316	  fn.setLockProbe = (/** @type {() => boolean} */ f) => { lockProbeRef.fn = f; };
  1317	  return fn;
  1318	}
  1319

  1324	    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];

    86	function ownedBundle(originPlatform = 'claude-code', status = 'open') {
    87	  return {

    96	    decisions: [],

   100	    handoff:
   101	      status === 'sealed'
   102	        ? { status: 'sealed', reason: 'seal', reasonClass: 'usage-limit', toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: T0, receive_log: [] }
   103	        : { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] },
   104	    journalSeq: 0,

   110	/**
   111	 * A STRICT, stateful fake git: unstubbed commands reject; worktree add/list and
   112	 * checkout -b are modeled so setup + preflight/postflight + merge cohere.
   113	 * `over` overrides specific responses (e.g. a merge conflict).
   114	 */
   115	function pipelineGit({ over = {}, log = CLEAN_LOG, existingBranches = [], dirtyCwds = [], mergedBranches = [] } = {}) {
   116	  const added = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // path -> branch
   117	  const branchAt = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // cwd -> current branch
   118	  const branches = new Set(existingBranches); // stale/pre-existing namespaced branches
   119	  const dirty = new Set(dirtyCwds); // seat cwds whose index is dirty
   120	  const deleted = new Set(); // seats listed by git but raw-deleted on disk
   121	  const merged = new Set(mergedBranches); // branches already merged into main (ancestor; empty main..branch)
   122	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   123	  const lockProbeRef = { fn: /** @type {null | (() => boolean)} */ (null) };
   124	  let mainSha = MAIN_SHA; // mutable so a child can "move main" mid-subtask
   125	
   126	  const list = () => {
   127	    const entries = [{ path: '/repo', branch: 'main' }];
   128	    for (const [path, branch] of added) entries.push({ path, branch: branchAt.get(path) ?? branch });
   129	    return entries.map((e) => `worktree ${e.path}\nHEAD ${MAIN_SHA}\nbranch refs/heads/${e.branch}\n`).join('\n');
   130	  };
   131	
   132	  const fn = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   133	    const argstr = args.join(' ');
   134	    calls.push({ cmd, args, argstr, cwd: opts?.cwd, lockPresent: lockProbeRef.fn ? lockProbeRef.fn() : undefined });
   135	    if (cmd !== 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
   136	
   137	    // Explicit overrides win.
   138	    const okey = Object.keys(over).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length).find((k) => argstr.includes(k));
   139	    if (okey) {
   140	      const r = over[okey];
   141	      if (r?.reject) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(r.stderr || 'git failed'), { code: r.code ?? 1, stdout: r.stdout ?? '', stderr: r.stderr ?? '' }));
   142	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: r?.stdout ?? '', stderr: r?.stderr ?? '' });
   143	    }
   144	
   145	    // A raw-deleted seat: git still LISTS it, but any command IN that cwd fails
   146	    // (the directory is gone). Only self-heal (prune + re-add) recovers it.
   147	    if (opts?.cwd && deleted.has(opts.cwd) && !argstr.startsWith('worktree ')) {
   148	      return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: cannot chdir to '${opts.cwd}': No such file or directory`), { code: 128, stderr: 'No such file or directory\n' }));
   149	    }
   150	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree list')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: list(), stderr: '' });
   151	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree add')) {
   152	      const path = args.find((a) => a.startsWith('/repo/.worktrees/'));
   153	      const bi = args.indexOf('-b');
   154	      const branch = bi >= 0 ? args[bi + 1] : 'baton/wt-x/base';
   155	      if (path) { added.set(path, branch); branchAt.set(path, branch); deleted.delete(path); }
   156	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   157	    }
   158	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree prune')) { deleted.clear(); return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }); }
   159	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree remove')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   160	    if (argstr.startsWith('checkout -b')) {
   161	      const target = args[args.indexOf('-b') + 1];
   162	      if (branches.has(target)) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists`), { code: 128, stderr: `fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists\n` }));
   163	      branches.add(target);
   164	      if (opts?.cwd) branchAt.set(opts.cwd, target);
   165	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   166	    }
   167	    if (argstr.startsWith('status --porcelain')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: dirty.has(opts?.cwd) ? ' M dirty.txt\n' : '', stderr: '' });
   168	    if (argstr === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD') return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${branchAt.get(opts?.cwd) ?? 'main'}\n`, stderr: '' });
   169	    if (argstr.startsWith('rev-parse')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${mainSha}\n`, stderr: '' });
   170	    // merge-base --is-ancestor <branch> main : exit 0 iff <branch> is already in main.
   171	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge-base --is-ancestor')) {
   172	      const b = args[args.indexOf('--is-ancestor') + 1];
   173	      return merged.has(b) ? Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }) : Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('not an ancestor'), { code: 1, stdout: '', stderr: '' }));
   174	    }
   175	    if (argstr.startsWith('log ')) {
   176	      // A merged branch has NO commits ahead of main (empty main..<branch>).
   177	      const range = args.find((a) => /\.\./.test(String(a))) ?? '';
   178	      const branch = String(range).split('..')[1];
   179	      if (branch && merged.has(branch)) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   180	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: log, stderr: '' });
   181	    }
   182	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'Fast-forward', stderr: '' });
   183	    if (argstr.startsWith('branch -D')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   184	    return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`unstubbed git: ${argstr}`), { code: 'ENOSTUB' }));
   185	  };
   186	  fn.calls = calls;
   187	  fn.git = () => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git');
   188	  fn.issued = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.some((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   189	  fn.matching = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   190	  fn.withCwd = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re, /** @type {string} */ cwd) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr) && c.cwd === cwd);
   191	  fn.setLockProbe = (/** @type {() => boolean} */ f) => { lockProbeRef.fn = f; };
   192	  fn.moveMain = (/** @type {string} */ sha) => { mainSha = sha; }; // a child "moves main" mid-subtask
   193	  fn.setBranch = (/** @type {string} */ cwd, /** @type {string} */ branch) => { branchAt.set(cwd, branch); };
   194	  // Explicit seed: git LISTS this worktree; `raw` marks its dir as deleted on
   195	  // disk (any in-cwd command fails until prune + re-add self-heals it).
   196	  fn.seedWorktree = (/** @type {string} */ path, /** @type {string} */ branch, /** @type {{raw?: boolean}} */ o = {}) => {
   197	    added.set(path, branch);
   198	    branchAt.set(path, branch);
   199	    if (o.raw) deleted.add(path);
   200	  };
   201	  return fn;
   202	}
   203	
   204	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
   205	  const queue = [...script];
   206	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   207	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   208	    calls.push({ spec, opts, command: spec?.command, args: spec?.args ?? [], env: spec?.env });
   209	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { verdict: 'APPROVED' };
   210	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string' && opts.logPath) {
   211	      const dir = opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   212	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   213	      io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, r.logContent);
   214	    }
   215	    // A child may perturb the world (e.g. move main) between spawn and postflight.
   216	    if (typeof r.effect === 'function') r.effect();
   217	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath: opts.logPath });
   218	  };
   219	  fn.calls = calls;
   220	  return fn;

+// ===========================================================================
+// ITEM 2 (v1.1) — trunk derivation. Replace hardcoded 'main' with the repo's
+// actual default branch (git symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD → fallback
+// rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD at setup), recorded in state and reused, never
+// re-derived mid-run. Pinned in a MASTER-trunk fake repo — the observable is
+// "master used everywhere; one derivation", not the exact command.
+
+// A fake git modeling a MASTER-trunk repo: the default branch is 'master'
+// (symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD → origin/master; fallback rev-parse
+// --abbrev-ref HEAD at the ROOT → master). It answers 'main' refs LENIENTLY
+// (same sha/ranges) so a main-hardcoded impl still runs end to end — the pin's
+// teeth are that NO 'main' appears in the recorded git argv. deriveThrows makes
+// the default-branch derivation fail (to prove a resume reuses a stamped trunk).
+function masterGit({ merged = [], existingBranches = [], dirtyCwds = [], log = CLEAN_LOG, deriveThrows = false } = {}) {
+  const added = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map());
+  const branchAt = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map());
+  const branches = new Set(existingBranches);
+  const dirty = new Set(dirtyCwds);
+  const mergedSet = new Set(merged);
+  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
+  const lockProbeRef = { fn: /** @type {null | (() => boolean)} */ (null) };
+  const list = () => {
+    const entries = [{ path: '/repo', branch: 'master' }];
+    for (const [path, branch] of added) entries.push({ path, branch: branchAt.get(path) ?? branch });
+    return entries.map((e) => `worktree ${e.path}\nHEAD ${MAIN_SHA}\nbranch refs/heads/${e.branch}\n`).join('\n');
+  };
+  const fn = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
+    const argstr = args.join(' ');
+    calls.push({ cmd, args, argstr, cwd: opts?.cwd, lockPresent: lockProbeRef.fn ? lockProbeRef.fn() : undefined });
+    if (cmd !== 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
+    // ---- trunk derivation (both forms) ----
+    if (argstr === 'symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD') {
+      if (deriveThrows) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('fatal: no origin/HEAD'), { code: 128, stderr: 'no origin/HEAD\n' }));
+      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'refs/remotes/origin/master\n', stderr: '' });
+    }
+    if (argstr === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD') {
+      // LEGIT seat HEAD probes (alreadyOnBranch / preflight / postflight) run in
+      // a seat cwd and return that seat's branch (never 'master' — a pre-checkout
+      // probe yields the seat base, so it can never be mistaken for the trunk).
+      if (opts?.cwd === WT_A || opts?.cwd === WT_B) {
+        const dflt = opts.cwd === WT_A ? 'baton/wt-a/base' : 'baton/wt-b/base';
+        return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${branchAt.get(opts.cwd) ?? dflt}\n`, stderr: '' });
+      }
+      // Anything else — the root fallback OR a STRAY probe in any other cwd — is
+      // a trunk-derivation attempt: it fails under deriveThrows and is counted as
+      // a derivation call (so a seat-cwd re-derivation cannot slip past).
+      if (deriveThrows) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('fatal: HEAD'), { code: 128, stderr: 'fatal: not a HEAD\n' }));
+      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'master\n', stderr: '' });
+    }
+    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree list')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: list(), stderr: '' });
+    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree add')) {
+      const path = args.find((a) => a.startsWith('/repo/.worktrees/'));
+      const bi = args.indexOf('-b');
+      const branch = bi >= 0 ? args[bi + 1] : 'baton/wt-x/base';
+      if (path) { added.set(path, branch); branchAt.set(path, branch); }
+      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
+    }
+    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree prune') || argstr.startsWith('worktree remove')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
+    if (argstr.startsWith('checkout -b')) {
+      const target = args[args.indexOf('-b') + 1];
+      if (branches.has(target)) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists`), { code: 128, stderr: `fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists\n` }));
+      branches.add(target);
+      if (opts?.cwd) branchAt.set(opts.cwd, target);
+      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
+    }
+    if (argstr.startsWith('status --porcelain')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: dirty.has(opts?.cwd) ? ' M dirty.txt\n' : '', stderr: '' });
+    // merge-base --is-ancestor <branch> <trunk>: exit 0 iff branch already merged.
+    if (argstr.startsWith('merge-base --is-ancestor')) {
+      const b = args[args.indexOf('--is-ancestor') + 1];
+      return mergedSet.has(b) ? Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }) : Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('not an ancestor'), { code: 1, stdout: '', stderr: '' }));
+    }
+    // Lenient: rev-parse of ANY single ref (master OR main) resolves to the sha.
+    if (argstr.startsWith('rev-parse')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${MAIN_SHA}\n`, stderr: '' });
+    if (argstr.startsWith('log ')) {
+      const range = args.find((a) => /\.\./.test(String(a))) ?? '';
+      const branch = String(range).split('..')[1];
+      if (branch && mergedSet.has(branch)) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }); // merged → empty
+      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: log, stderr: '' });
+    }
+    if (argstr.startsWith('merge')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'Fast-forward', stderr: '' }); // merge <branch> and merge --ff-only <trunk>
+    if (argstr.startsWith('branch -D')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
+    return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`unstubbed git: ${argstr}`), { code: 'ENOSTUB' }));
+  };
+  fn.calls = calls;
+  fn.git = () => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git');
+  fn.mainCalls = () => fn.git().filter((c) => c.argstr.includes('main'));
+  // A trunk-derivation attempt = symbolic-ref origin/HEAD OR any abbrev-ref HEAD
+  // that is NOT one of the known legit SEAT probes (WT_A/WT_B). This counts a
+  // seat-cwd re-derivation too — the exclusion is the explicit seat allowlist,
+  // never a blanket "non-root" filter — so "derive once, never re-derive" is
+  // pinned by the count, not just the observable trunk.
+  fn.derivationCalls = () =>
+    fn.git().filter(
+      (c) => c.argstr === 'symbolic-ref refs/remotes/origin/HEAD' || (c.argstr === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD' && c.cwd !== WT_A && c.cwd !== WT_B),
+    ).length;
+  // The legit seat HEAD probes the pipeline makes (alreadyOnBranch / preflight /
+  // postflight), confined to the two seat cwds — asserted explicitly.
+  fn.seatProbes = () => fn.git().filter((c) => c.argstr === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD' && (c.cwd === WT_A || c.cwd === WT_B));
+  fn.setLockProbe = (/** @type {() => boolean} */ f) => { lockProbeRef.fn = f; };
+  return fn;
+}
+
+describe('pipeline — trunk derivation (v1.1 item 2)', () => {
+  const MERGES = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/merges.ndjson`;
+  const mergeReceiptsOf = (io) => {
+    const raw = io.files()[MERGES];
+    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
+  };
+
+  it('RED (trunk-1): a master-trunk pipeline completes DONE using master everywhere — no "main" in any git argv; prompts range over master', async () => {
+    const git = masterGit();
+    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), git, runner: undefined });
+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
+    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 0, `a master-trunk run completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
+    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the run reached DONE');
+    // Teeth: NO trunk touchpoint used the hardcoded 'main'.
+    assert.deepEqual(git.mainCalls().map((c) => c.argstr), [], 'every trunk git call targets master — no main anywhere');
+    // Reviewer prompt ranges over master..branch.
+    const rev = reviewersOf(io.__runner)[0];
+    assert.ok(rev, 'a reviewer ran');
+    const revPrompt = argsOf(rev).join(' ');
+    assert.match(revPrompt, /git diff master\.\.baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1/, 'the reviewer prompt ranges over master..<branch>');
+    assert.doesNotMatch(revPrompt, /\bmain\.\./, 'the reviewer prompt never ranges over main');
+    // Merger prompt names master as the trunk.
+    const merger = io.__runner.calls.find((c) => argsOf(c).join(' ').includes('You are the merger'));
+    assert.ok(merger, 'a merger ran');
+    assert.match(argsOf(merger).join(' '), /against master/, 'the merger prompt names master as the trunk');
+    // Receipt shape unchanged.
+    assert.ok(
+      mergeReceiptsOf(io).some((r) => r.subtaskId === 't1' && r.branch === 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1' && typeof r.mergedAt === 'string'),
+      'the merge receipt keeps its {subtaskId, branch, mergedAt} shape',
+    );
+  });
+
+  it('RED (trunk-2): the stale-branch park message names the trunk (git log master..<branch>), not main', async () => {
+    const branch = 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1';
+    const git = masterGit({ merged: [branch] }); // is-ancestor true, empty master..branch, and NO receipt → stale park
+    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), git, runner: undefined });
+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
+    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 4, 'an ancestor-without-receipt stale branch parks');
+    const out = io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText();
+    assert.match(out, /git log master\.\.baton\/wt-a\/subtask-t1/, 'the stale-park message points at git log master..<branch>');
+    assert.doesNotMatch(out, /\bmain\.\./, 'the stale-park message never ranges over main');
+  });
+
+  it('RED (trunk-3a): a fresh master-trunk run STAMPS the derived trunk into state.json', async () => {
+    const git = masterGit();
+    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks: [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }] }), git, runner: undefined });
+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
+    await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
+    const state = JSON.parse(io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').state]);
+    assert.equal(state.trunk, 'master', 'the derived trunk is persisted in state.json for reuse');
+    assert.equal(git.derivationCalls(), 1, 'the trunk is derived EXACTLY once at init (one root-level derivation)');
+  });
+
+  it('RED (trunk-3b): resume REUSES the stamped trunk and does not re-derive (completes even when derivation now fails)', async () => {
+    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
+    const git = masterGit({ deriveThrows: true }); // deriving would fail now — the resume must not need it
+    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
+    // A prior run already derived + stamped the trunk.
+    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1, status: 'running', trunk: 'master' });
+    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 0, `resume completes using the stamped trunk without re-deriving; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
+    assert.equal((await loadLoopState('/repo', io)).state.status, LOOP_STATUS.DONE, 'the resumed run reached DONE');
+    assert.deepEqual(git.mainCalls().map((c) => c.argstr), [], 'the resumed run uses the stamped master everywhere');
+    // Derive-once teeth: a stamped resume issues NO derivation — not symbolic-ref,
+    // not a root fallback, and not a stray abbrev-ref in any non-seat cwd (a
+    // seat-cwd re-derivation would count here too, since only WT_A/WT_B probes
+    // are excluded).
+    assert.equal(git.derivationCalls(), 0, 'a stamped resume NEVER re-derives the trunk (not even to catch-and-fallback, and not via a seat cwd)');
+    // The exclusion is explicit: the only abbrev-ref HEAD calls are the known
+    // legit seat probes, confined to the two seat cwds.
+    assert.ok(git.seatProbes().length >= 1, 'the resume made its legit seat HEAD probes');
+    assert.ok(git.seatProbes().every((c) => c.cwd === WT_A || c.cwd === WT_B), 'every abbrev-ref HEAD probe is a seat probe (WT_A/WT_B), never a disguised derivation');
+  });
+
+  it('RED (trunk-4): a stamped state WITHOUT a trunk field migrates — derives once and stamps it, never a refusal', async () => {
+    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'only' }];
+    const git = masterGit();
+    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask());
+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
+    // A pre-trunk stamped state: flavor + specDigest present, but NO trunk field.
+    // Missing trunk alone is NOT the I3 legacy-unstamped refusal.
+    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 1, status: 'running' });
+    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
+    assert.notEqual(code, 2, 'a missing trunk field is additive migration, not a legacy-unstamped refusal');
+    assert.equal(code, 0, `the run migrates and completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
+    const state = JSON.parse(io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').state]);
+    assert.equal(state.trunk, 'master', 'the trunk is derived once and stamped on migration');
+    assert.equal(git.derivationCalls(), 1, 'migration derives the trunk EXACTLY once, then stamps it');
+  });
+
+  it('RED (trunk-5): a receipt-backed already-merged subtask auto-advances under master (no t1 writer), zero main in argv', async () => {
+    const subtasks = [{ id: 't1', title: 'first' }, { id: 't2', title: 'second' }];
+    const t1Branch = 'baton/wt-a/subtask-t1';
+    // t1 is genuinely merged under master: is-ancestor true, empty master..t1,
+    // AND a receipt. t2 is unmerged and runs normally.
+    const git = masterGit({ merged: [t1Branch] });
+    const io = makePipeRepo({ spec: pipelineSpec({ subtasks }), git, runner: undefined });
+    io.superviseChild = fakeRunner(io, cleanSubtask()); // enough for t2 only
+    io.__runner = io.superviseChild;
+    seedPipelineState(io, { flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest: dedupeKey(subtasks), phaseIndex: 0, phaseCount: 2, status: 'running', trunk: 'master' });
+    io.fs.mkdirSync(loopPaths('/repo').dir, { recursive: true });
+    io.fs.writeFileSync(MERGES, JSON.stringify({ subtaskId: 't1', branch: t1Branch, mergedAt: T0 }) + '\n');
+
+    const code = await run(['pipeline', 'run'], io);
+    assert.equal(code, 0, `the receipt-backed t1 auto-advances and t2 completes; stderr: ${io.stderrText()}`);
+    assert.doesNotMatch(io.stderrText() + io.stdoutText(), /EMPTY branch|nothing to review or merge|stale/i, 'a receipt-backed merged subtask is not mistaken for empty/stale under master');
+    const writers = writersOf(io.__runner);
+    assert.ok(writers.length >= 1, 'the unmerged subtask still runs a writer');
+    assert.equal(valAfter(argsOf(writers[0]), '-C'), WT_B, 'the first writer is t2 (wt-b) — receipt-backed t1 was recognized as merged and skipped');
+    assert.ok(!writers.some((c) => argsOf(c).join(' ').includes("subtask 't1'")), 'no writer ran for the already-merged t1');
+    assert.deepEqual(git.mainCalls().map((c) => c.argstr), [], 'the already-merged recognizer and merge path use master — no main anywhere');
+  });

    38	const STRING_FLAGS = new Set(['root']);

    42	/**
    43	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    44	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    45	 */
    46	export async function cmdPipeline(args, io) {
    47	  /** @type {string[]} */
    48	  const positionals = [];
    49	  /** @type {string[]} */
    50	  const flagTokens = [];
    51	  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
    52	    const a = args[i];
    53	    if (typeof a === 'string' && a.startsWith('--')) {
    54	      flagTokens.push(a);
    55	      if (STRING_FLAGS.has(a.slice(2)) && i + 1 < args.length) {
    56	        i += 1;
    57	        flagTokens.push(args[i]);
    58	      }
    59	    } else {
    60	      positionals.push(a);
    61	    }
    62	  }
    63	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(flagTokens, {});
    64	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'pipeline', parsed.error);
    65	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    66	
    67	  const sub = positionals[0];
    68	  if (sub === undefined) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', 'a subcommand is required — try: baton pipeline run');
    69	  if (sub !== 'run' && sub !== 'resume') return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `unknown subcommand '${sub}' (supported: run, resume)`);
    70	  if (positionals.length > 1) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `unexpected argument '${positionals[1]}'`);
    71	  if (sub === 'resume') flags.__resume = true;
    72	
    73	  return runPipeline(flags, io);
    74	}
    75	
    76	/** Validate the spec's subtasks — every rejection names its offender.
    77	 * @param {any} subtasks @returns {string | null} */
    78	function validateSubtasks(subtasks) {
    79	  if (!Array.isArray(subtasks) || subtasks.length === 0) {
    80	    return 'loop.json needs a non-empty `subtasks` array ([{id, title}]) for a pipeline run';
    81	  }
    82	  const seen = new Set();
    83	  for (let i = 0; i < subtasks.length; i += 1) {
    84	    const st = subtasks[i];
    85	    if (typeof st?.id !== 'string' || st.id.length === 0) return `subtasks[${i}] is missing a string id`;
    86	    if (typeof st?.title !== 'string' || st.title.length === 0) return `subtasks[${i}] ('${st.id}') is missing a string title`;
    87	    if (seen.has(st.id)) return `subtasks carry a duplicate id '${st.id}' — ids name branches and must be unique`;
    88	    seen.add(st.id);
    89	  }
    90	  return null;
    91	}
    92	
    93	/**
    94	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags @param {any} io
    95	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    96	 */
    97	async function runPipeline(flags, io) {
    98	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
    99	  const p = loopPaths(root);
   100	
   101	  const rawSpec = safeReadJson(io.fs, `${root}/loop.json`);
   102	  if (!rawSpec.ok) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `no loop.json at ${root} — scaffold one with: baton loop init "<goal>"`);
   103	  const spec = rawSpec.value;
   104	  const subtaskError = validateSubtasks(spec?.subtasks);
   105	  if (subtaskError !== null) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', subtaskError);
   106	  const { config } = loadConfig(root, io);
   107	  if (!config) return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `no readable baton.config.json at ${root} — the role matrix is required`);
   108	  // The 5-iteration cap is a hard invariant (Gate-2 fold G2): a spec asking
   109	  // for more is rejected BEFORE anything spawns.
   110	  const rawCap = spec?.budgets?.iterationCap;
   111	  if (rawCap !== undefined && (typeof rawCap !== 'number' || rawCap < 1 || rawCap > 5)) {
   112	    return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `budgets.iterationCap must be a number between 1 and 5 (the 5-iteration cap is a hard invariant; got ${JSON.stringify(rawCap)})`);
   113	  }
   114	  const cap = typeof rawCap === 'number' ? rawCap : 5;
   115	  const timeoutMs = (typeof spec?.budgets?.perRoleTimeoutMin === 'number' ? spec.budgets.perRoleTimeoutMin : 30) * 60_000;
   116	
   117	  // One supervisor at a time — the same atomic run lock as `loop run`, with
   118	  // dead-owner reclaim + orphan reaping (Gate-2 fold G2/B8).
   119	  const lock = await acquireSupervisorLock(root, io, 'pipeline run');
   120	  if (lock.ok !== true) return lock.code;
   121	  try {
   122	    return await drivePipeline(flags, io, { root, p, spec, config, cap, timeoutMs });
   123	  } finally {
   124	    lock.release();
   125	  }
   126	}
   127	
   128	/**
   129	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags @param {any} io
   130	 * @param {{root: string, p: any, spec: any, config: any, cap: number, timeoutMs: number}} ctx
   131	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
   132	 */
   133	async function drivePipeline(flags, io, { root, p, spec, config, cap, timeoutMs }) {
   134	  const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
   135	  /** @param {string} role @param {Array<{platform: string, model: string}>} [avoidEntries] @returns {any | null} */
   136	  const resolveOne = (role, avoidEntries = []) => {
   137	    const a = resolveRoles({ config, to: 'claude-code', avoid: [], avoidEntries, probes: null }).assignments[role];
   138	    return a && a.mode !== 'unavailable' ? a : null;
   139	  };
   140	
   141	  // The synthesized phase list makes the loop state machine (phase count,
   142	  // done detection, journal replay) work unchanged for the subtask cycle.
   143	  const subtasks = spec.subtasks;
   144	  const stateSpec = {
   145	    goal: spec.goal,
   146	    phases: subtasks.map((/** @type {any} */ st, /** @type {number} */ i) => ({ id: `subtask-${st.id}`, role: `worker-${i % 2 === 0 ? 'a' : 'b'}` })),
   147	  };
   148	  // RESUME, never re-init: a prior run's cap counters and position survive a
   149	  // re-invocation (Gate-2 fold G2 — no cap refunds). The state is stamped
   150	  // {flavor, specDigest} so a cross-flavor or changed-spec resume refuses
   151	  // instead of misaligning (H5).
   152	  const specDigest = dedupeKey(subtasks);
   153	  /** @type {any} */
   154	  let state = (await loadLoopState(root, io)).state;
   155	  if (state === null) {
   156	    // A resume with nothing persisted is a wrong-directory mistake, not a
   157	    // fresh run — refusing beats silently initializing and running (N2).
   158	    if (flags.__resume === true) {
   159	      return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', 'nothing to resume — no run state exists here; start with: baton pipeline run');
   160	    }
   161	    state = { ...initLoopState(stateSpec, io), flavor: 'pipeline', specDigest };
   162	    await writeLoopState(root, state, io);
   163	  } else {
   164	    if (typeof state.flavor === 'string' && state.flavor !== 'pipeline') {
   165	      return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', `the persisted run state is flavor '${state.flavor}' — a ${state.flavor} run cannot be resumed as a pipeline (flavor mismatch); archive .handoff/loop or finish the ${state.flavor} run first`);
   166	    }
   167	    // A missing stamp is a mismatch, not a grandfathered pass — pre-stamp
   168	    // legacy state resumes with exactly the misalignment H5 prevents (I3).
   169	    if (typeof state.flavor !== 'string' || typeof state.specDigest !== 'string') {
   170	      return usageError(io, flags, 'pipeline', 'the persisted run state is missing its {flavor, specDigest} stamp (pre-stamp legacy state) — resuming it could misalign the run; archive .handoff/loop to start fresh');

    30	/** @param {string} root */
    31	export function worktreePaths(root) {
    32	  const dir = `${root}/.worktrees`;
    33	  /** @type {Record<string, string>} */
    34	  const seats = { a: `${dir}/wt-a`, b: `${dir}/wt-b` };
    35	  return { dir, seats };
    36	}

    39	const seatBranch = (seat) => `baton/wt-${seat}/base`;

    41	/** @param {any} io @param {string} cwd @param {string[]} args */
    42	async function git(io, cwd, args) {
    43	  return io.execFile('git', args, { cwd });
    44	}
    45	
    46	/** Parse `git worktree list --porcelain` into [{path, branch}]. @param {string} out */
    47	function parseWorktreeList(out) {
    48	  /** @type {Array<{path: string, branch: string | null}>} */
    49	  const entries = [];
    50	  for (const block of String(out).split(/\n\n+/)) {
    51	    const path = block.match(/^worktree (.+)$/m)?.[1];
    52	    if (!path) continue;
    53	    const branch = block.match(/^branch refs\/heads\/(.+)$/m)?.[1] ?? null;
    54	    entries.push({ path, branch });
    55	  }
    56	  return entries;
    57	}
    58	
    59	/**
    60	 * Ensure both seat worktrees exist on their namespaced branches and that
    61	 * `.worktrees/` is gitignored. Idempotent: an existing seat is left alone.
    62	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
    63	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, worktrees: Array<{seat: string, path: string, branch: string}>, gitignoreChanged: boolean}>}
    64	 */
    65	export async function setupWorktrees(root, io) {
    66	  const p = worktreePaths(root);
    67	
    68	  const gitignorePath = `${root}/.gitignore`;
    69	  const existing = io.fs.existsSync(gitignorePath) ? io.fs.readFileSync(gitignorePath, 'utf8') : '';
    70	  const ensured = ensureIgnoreLine(existing, '.worktrees/');
    71	  if (ensured.changed) io.fs.writeFileSync(gitignorePath, ensured.text);
    72	
    73	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
    74	  /** @type {Array<{seat: string, path: string, branch: string}>} */
    75	  const worktrees = [];
    76	  for (const seat of SEATS) {
    77	    const path = p.seats[seat];
    78	    const present = listed.find((e) => e.path === path);
    79	    if (present) {
    80	      worktrees.push({ seat, path, branch: present.branch ?? seatBranch(seat) });
    81	      continue;
    82	    }
    83	    const branch = seatBranch(seat);
    84	    await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'add', '-b', branch, path]);
    85	    worktrees.push({ seat, path, branch });
    86	  }
    87	  return { ok: true, worktrees, gitignoreChanged: ensured.changed };
    88	}

   132	
   133	/**
   134	 * Full-range attribution scan over main..branch — the merge gate. Every
   135	 * commit must be authored AND committed by the sole author, with no forbidden
   136	 * AI-attribution trailer in the body. The range is never windowed.
   137	 * @param {string} root @param {string} branch @param {any} io
   138	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true} | {ok: false, offending: string, reason: string}>}
   139	 */
   140	export async function attributionScan(root, branch, io) {
   141	  const out = (await git(io, root, ['log', `main..${branch}`, `--format=${LOG_FORMAT}`])).stdout;
   142	  for (const record of String(out).split('\x1e')) {
   143	    if (record.trim().length === 0) continue;
   144	    const [sha, an, ae, cn, ce, body = ''] = record.replace(/^\n/, '').split('\x00');
   145	    if (an !== SOLE_AUTHOR.name || ae !== SOLE_AUTHOR.email) {
   146	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} has a foreign author identity (${an} <${ae}>) — sole-author is required` };
   147	    }
   148	    if (cn !== SOLE_AUTHOR.name || ce !== SOLE_AUTHOR.email) {
   149	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} has a foreign committer identity (${cn} <${ce}>) — sole-author is required` };
   150	    }
   151	    if (TRAILER_PATTERNS.some((re) => re.test(body))) {
   152	      return { ok: false, offending: sha, reason: `commit ${sha} carries a forbidden AI-attribution trailer (co-authored/generated-with/emoji) in its message` };
   153	    }
   154	  }
   155	  return { ok: true };
   156	}
   157	
   158	const mergeLockPath = (/** @type {string} */ root) => `${root}/.handoff/loop/merge.lock`;
   159	
   160	/**
   161	 * Merge an approved subtask branch into main — one guarded transaction:
   162	 * acquire the merge lock (a held lock refuses), gate on the attribution scan,
   163	 * merge at the repo root, abort-and-park on conflict, then ff-only sync both
   164	 * seats. The lock is released on EVERY exit path after acquisition.
   165	 * @param {string} root @param {{branch: string}} input @param {any} io
   166	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: true, branch: string} | {ok: false, reason: string}>}
   167	 */
   168	export async function mergeSubtask(root, input, io) {
   169	  const lock = mergeLockPath(root);
   170	  if (io.fs.existsSync(lock)) {
   171	    return { ok: false, reason: `the merge lock at ${lock} is held by another merger — refusing a concurrent merge` };
   172	  }
   173	  ensureDir(io.fs, `${root}/.handoff/loop`);
   174	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, lock, { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, at: io.now() });
   175	  try {
   176	    const scan = await attributionScan(root, input.branch, io);
   177	    if (scan.ok !== true) return { ok: false, reason: scan.reason };
   178	
   179	    try {
   180	      await git(io, root, ['merge', input.branch]);
   181	    } catch (err) {
   182	      // Conflict (or any merge failure): abort, never auto-resolve — park.
   183	      try {
   184	        await git(io, root, ['merge', '--abort']);
   185	      } catch {
   186	        // Nothing to abort — the failure predates a merge state.
   187	      }
   188	      const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.stdout || /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message || String(err);
   189	      return { ok: false, reason: `merge conflict — aborted and parked for the operator: ${String(msg).split('\n')[0]}` };
   190	    }
   191	
   192	    const seats = worktreePaths(root).seats;
   193	    for (const seat of SEATS) {
   194	      try {
   195	        await git(io, seats[seat], ['merge', '--ff-only', 'main']);
   196	      } catch (err) {
   197	        const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.stderr || /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message || String(err);
   198	        return { ok: false, reason: `worktree wt-${seat} could not fast-forward to main — corruption signal, parked: ${String(msg).split('\n')[0]}` };
   199	      }
   200	    }
   201	    return { ok: true, branch: input.branch };
   202	  } finally {
   203	    try {
   204	      if (io.fs.existsSync(lock)) io.fs.unlinkSync(lock);
   205	    } catch {
   206	      // Best effort — a leaked lock is visible and operator-recoverable.
   207	    }
   208	  }
   209	}
   210	
   211	/**
   212	 * Self-heal a raw-deleted seat: git still lists it but the directory is gone
   213	 * — prune the stale metadata and re-add on the same branch.
   214	 * @param {string} root @param {{seat: string, branch: string}} target @param {any} io
   215	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, healed: boolean}>}
   216	 */
   217	export async function selfHealWorktree(root, target, io) {
   218	  const path = worktreePaths(root).seats[target.seat];
   219	  const listed = parseWorktreeList((await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'list', '--porcelain'])).stdout);
   220	  const stale = listed.some((e) => e.path === path) && !io.fs.existsSync(path);
   221	  if (!stale) return { ok: true, healed: false };
   222	  await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'prune']);
   223	  await git(io, root, ['worktree', 'add', path, target.branch]);
   224	  return { ok: true, healed: true };
   225	}
   226	
   227	/**
   228	 * Tear down BOTH seats: remove the worktrees and delete their namespaced
   229	 * branches. Anything outside .worktrees/wt-* or baton/wt-* is never touched,
   230	 * so a teardown leaves the repo a plain git repo (acceptance constraint 5).
   231	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   232	 * @returns {Promise<{ok: boolean, removed: string[]}>}
   233	 */
   234	export async function teardownWorktrees(root, io) {
   235	  const p = worktreePaths(root);

exec
